ORDER 


New Delhi , the 19th May , 1981 


नई विरुली, 19 मई, 1981 
का०आ०1665/ माई. डी०पार०ए० / 6/ 4/78 - विकास परिषद ( प्रश्यिा 
संबधी ) नियम 1952 के मियम 2 और 4 के साथ पठित उद्योग (विकास 
एव विनियमन ) अधिनियम , 1951 ( 1951 का 65 ) की धारा 6 द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियो का प्रयोग करते हुए केसीय सरकार एतदद्वारा कानिक 
रमायन उधार के लिये विकास परिषद के एक सदस्य डा० एस० वरदराजन , 
अध्यक्ष एवं प्रबन्ध निदेशक , इडियन पेट्रो केमिकल्स कारपोरेशन लि . , 
बौवा ( गुजरात ) को उक्त विकास परिषद् का उपाध्यक्ष नियुक्त करती 
है और निदेश देती है कि भारत मरकार उद्योग महालय ( प्रौद्योगिक 
विकाम विभाग ) के दिमाक 30 अक्तूबर, 1979 के मानेश सख्या का० 
पा० प्राई ०डी०मार०६० 6/ 4/ 78 मे निम्नलिखित संशाधन किये जायंगे , 
अर्थात् :--- 

( 1 ) श्रम स० 1 प्रार उसकी प्रविष्टिया के बाद निम्नलिखित 
प्रविष्टियो जोडी जाएगी मर्थात् - -- 

डा . १० वरदराजन , उपासमा 
मध्यक्ष एवं प्रबंध निदेशक , जियन पेट्रोकेमिकल्स फारपोरेशन लि० , 
मड़ौदा ( गुजरात ) 
( ii ) क्रम स० 7 और उसकी प्रविष्टियां मिटा दी जायेगी । 

( iii ) क्रम स० 2, 3, 4, 5 और 6 को क्रमश क्रम स० 3, 4, 5 
पौर 7 के रूप मे पुन प्रषित किया जायेगा । 

[ सं० 8 ( 4 )/ 84-सी०डी०एन०] 
कमल कान्त असवाल, उप सभिव

confeiled by section 6 of the Industries (Development and

Central Government hereby appoints Dr S . Vardarajan, Chair

का . आ . 168 6) माईजी प्रार.०१/6/ 5. - केन्द्रीय सरकार, विकास परिषद् 
( प्रक्रिया ) मियम, 1952 के नियम 2, 4 और 5 के साप पठित उद्योग 
(विकास पौर विनियमन ) अधिनियम , 1951 ( 1951 का 65 ) की धारा 
6 द्वारा प्रवस शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व 
उद्योग पौर मार्गारक पूर्ति मन्त्रालय के मावेश सं० का०भा० 313 -- पाई/ 

०मार०ए० तारीख 10 अनवरी, 1975 के अधीम नियुक्त किए गए सवस्यों 
के , जिनकी पदावधि, प्रवधि खत्म हो जाने के कारण या अन्यथा समाप्त 
हो गई है, स्थान पर, विनिर्माण या उत्पादन में लगे हए अनुसूचित 
उद्योगों के लिए खाद्य प्रसंस्करण उद्योगों की विकास परिषद् के सदस्य 
होने के लिए, निम्नलिखित व्यक्तियों को राजपत्र में इस प्रादेस के प्रकामान 
फी तारीख से ही वो वर्ष की अवधि के लिए नियुक्त करती है : 

पाच प्रसंस्करण उद्योग विकास परिषम् ।

पेद्रोलियम , रसायन और उर्वरक मंत्रालय 

( पैट्रोलियम विभाग ) 

नई दिल्ली 20 मई, 1981 
का०मा०1667.-~-यत. केन्द्रीय गरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि 
लोकहित में यह पावश्यक है कि उसर प्रदेश में मथुरा से जलन्धर ( पंजाब ) 
तक पेट्रोलियम पदार्थों के परिवहन के लिए पाइप लाइन इंडियन प्रायल 
कारपोरेशन द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिये । 

और यत: यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाईनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतद्पाबद्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार 
अजित करना प्रावश्यक है । 

पनः, प्रन पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग 
के अधिकार वा प्रजन ) अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 
3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय 
परफार ने उसमें उपयोग वा अधिकार मर्जित करने का प्रपना पाशय 
एतद्धारा घोषित किया है । 

बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में हितबद्ध कोई व्यक्ति, उस भूमि के नीचे 
पाइप लाइन बिछाने के लिए प्राक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी, इंडियन ऑयल 
कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड, मथुग जलन्धर पाईप लाइन प्रोजेक्ट , म्यू हाऊस , 
कुम्जपुरा मार्ग, करमाल ( हरियाणा ) को इस अधिसूचना की तारीख 
से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा ।

MINISTRY OF PETROLEUM , CHEMICALS & 

FERTILIZER 
(Department of Petroleum ) 

New Delhi, the 20th May , 1981 
S . O . 1667. - Whereas it appears to the Central Govern 
ment that it is necessary in the public interest that for the 
tianaport of petroleum products from Mathura in Uttar 
Pradesh to Jullundur in Punjab pipelines should be laid by 
the Indian Oil Corporation Limited . 

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying such 
pipelines, it is necessary to acquire the Right of User In the 
fand described in the schedule annexed hereto : 

Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
Sub- section ( 1) of section 3 of the Petroleum and Minerals 
pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in land ) Act, 1962 
( 50 of 1962), the Central Government hereby declare its in 
tention to acquire the right of users therein : 

Provided that any person interested in the said land may, 
within 21 days from the date of this notification , object to 
the laying of the pipelines under the land to the Competent 
Authority , Indian Oil Corporation Limited , Mathura - Jullundur 
Pipeline New House, Kunjpura Road, Karnal ( Haryana ). 

And every person making such an objection shall also stato 
specifically whether he wishes to be heard in porson Jr by a 
legal practitioner .

नई दिल्ली, 20 मई, 1981 
मा० मा0 1688. - यन. केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोक 
हित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि उत्तर प्रदेश मे मथुरा से जालन्धर (पंजाब ) तक 
पेट्रोलियम पदार्थों के परिवहन के लिए पाईप लाइन इंडियन प्रॉयल कार्पो. 
रेशन द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिये । । 

और मत . यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाईनो को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतवपामय अनुसूची में गणित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित 
करमा आवश्यक है । 

मत प्रम पेट्रोलियम और बमिज पाप लाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकार का अजम ) अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रसस्त शक्तियो का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार ने 
उममें उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का प्रपना प्राशय एतद्वारा 
घोषित किया है । 

बर्से कि उक्त भूमि मे हिनबद्ध कोई व्यक्ति , उस भूमि के नीचे पाइप 
लाईन विधाने के लिए प्राक्षेप मलम प्राधिकारी, इंडियन ऑयल कार्पोरेशन 
लिमिटेड, मथुरा जलन्धर पाईप लाईन प्रायोजना, 705 मोती सिह नगर 
आलन्धर ( पंजाब ) को इम अधिसूचना की तारीख के 21 दिनों के भीतर 
कर सकेगा । 

और ऐसा प्राक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्मि विनिदिष्टत यह भी धन 
करेगा कि वह यह चाहना है कि उनकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिगत हो या किमी विधि 
म्यवसायी की मार्फत ।

केन्द्रीय सरकार ने पंजाब सरकार के परामर्श से डा० ( श्रीमती ) लीला रामा कुमार ,

फोहाली में स्थान पर भारतीय आयुर्विज्ञान परिषद् का सवस्य मनोनीत 
करती है ।

9 जनवरी , 1960 की अधिसूचना संख्या 5- 13/ 59 एम - 1 में निम्नलिखित 
और संशोधन करती है, अर्थात् :- -

रिशों पर विचार करने के बाव एतद्द्वारा इसके साथ लगी मनुसूची के कालम 2 में दी गई फिल्मों को उनके सभी भारतीय भाषामों के रूपान्तरों सहित ,

Short recital of the caso 


Shri Mahabir Sao was working as Surface Munshi in 
Totulmari colllery since take over of the colliery by tho Cen 
tral Government in 1976 . One Shri Basaval was detected 
to have been working in the name of Mahabir Sao , The 
Imposter found so working was stopped by the management 
vide letter No. TC / 73 / 20 /Pors / 76 dated 29 - 11- 76 from the 
Superintendent, Totulmari colliery . However, the genuine per 
son Mababir Sao who wag absopting was not availablo for 
duty . Subsequently tho union raised dispute that the genuine 
person Mahabir Sao was also stopped from work and de 
manded that he should be taken back in omployment . The 
mattor could not be resolved in course of conciliation pro 
ceedings . Subsequently the union raised dispute again before 
tho General Manager which was taken up for discussion in 
5 - 8 -80 with Shri Bindoshwari Dubey, President RCMS , In 
course of discussion it was agreed that if the management 
would fail to approve that Shri Mahabir Sao as an imposter 
within one month thery tho claimant should be taken back 
In employment. Accordingly the case of Shri Mahabir Sao 
was leferred to the District Magistrate Dhanbad vide this 
offico letter No, GM /SA / PD 143 / 5379 | 80 datert 20 /26 - 8 - 80 
for verification in order to prove identity of the person claim 
frrg to bo Mahabir Sgo . But so far nothing has been 
heard from the District Magistrate in this regard . On the 
other hand the union have been pressing for allowing Shri 
Mahabir Sao in terms of management s commitment in course 
of Union Managoment negotiation on 5 - 8 - 80 . The matter 
was again taken up for discussion with the Union and after 
discussion it was agreed to take Shri Mahabir Sao back in 
employment temporarily on the following terms and con 
ditions .

AWARD 


words the munshıs are told by the technical supervisory staff 
18 to from what place the miners of cach gang are to raise 
coal The report prepared by tho munsbi is in d piescribed 
form Even though the form is not a statutoiy one in one 
of its columns the munshi is to mention the place wherefrom 
tho coal is iaiscd On the basis of this report bills are pre 
pared and payments ATC made So it cannot be said that 
when the munshi fills the column himself for noting tho 
place wherefıom the coal is raised he does so without taking 
any responsibility of filling the column correctly The munshi 
report is signed by him in the present care the three con 
cerned workmen have signed their respective reports In the 
columns meant for noting down the place wherefrom the 
coal 18 talsed they have mentioned the place as main dip 10 
Seam So it in futile to argue that a munshi has no respon 
Bibility when he fills up this column, indicating the place 
wherefrom the coal is raised I have already said that the 
over- reporting by three concerned munshis may be with the 
knowledge of oi at the connivance of the miners , overman 
and mining sirdar But that would not exonerate the three 
concerned workmen when they have deliberately made false 
reports by over- reporting Thig act of their cannot but amount 
to dishonesty on their part Mr Boge in course of argument 
has diawn my attention to several provisions in the Coal 
Mines Regulations to show that it 19 the duty of the mining 
birdar as well as the overman to locate the place wherefrom 
coal 19 to be iaised and to take sufficient safety measures, 
But this does not mean that munshi 19 not to see the place 
wherefrom the coal is raised and to report blindly about the 
place in his report When the form used by the munghi has 
a column in which the place wherefrom the coal is raised has 
to be mentioned the munshi cannot escape his responsibility 
when he wrongly makes an entry in the column An Argu 
ment has been advanced by Mi Bose that Surveyoi s report 
which is said to be the basis of the charge against the thrce 
concerned workmen was not produced in the enquny But 
as I have already said the location and dimention of the 
place wherefrom coal was raised not being disputed , non 
production of surveyor s report in the enquy will not affect 
the merit of the case against the management A surveyor s 
report will only show the extent of drivago This is not dis 
puted as I have already pointed out Therefore , even accept 
ing the contention of Mr Bose that sui veyor s report was 
not produced in the enquiry it cannot be said that the man 
agement has not proved its case against the concerned work 
men Merely because other officers namely , the mining sirdar 
and the overman as contended by Mr Bosc either deliberately 
ovci looked the over- reporting or had any hand in the over 
reporting that would not be a circumstance to exonerate tho 
authors of the report, namely , the three concerned workmen , 
Assuming for the time being that other officers of the man 
agement aic also responsible for over -reporting committed 
by three concerned workmen that would not be an extenuat 
ing circumstance in favour of the concerned workmen Tho 
misconduct for which the thrce concerned workmen have 
been charged is of a very serious nature and certainly calls 
for punishment of severe type ic dismissal In my view the 
three concerned workmen have been lightly found guilty 
and have been rightly punished and the unpugncd order of 
punishment do not call for interference

cxercise of the powess conferred on them U / s 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of 
the Industrial Disputcs Act, 14 of 1947 have referred the 
following dispute to this Tribunal for adjudication by their

SCHEDULE

Issuc No. 1 : 

5 . From thc peruigal of the order of reference it would be 
found that the matter under reference is a collective dispute 
and is not covered by tho provisions of Section 2 - A of the 
I, D . Act. In view thereof it would follow that certainly it 
was essential that a formal dispute should have been raised 
and then and then only an Industrial Dispute couli be suid 
to have come into existence . There is nothing on record tc 
suggest even remotely that such a dispute was ever raised , 
In view thereof it would follow that the matter under reference 
does not qualify as an Industrial Dispute . From the perusal 
of para 2 of affidavit Ex. M . W . 111 it would he found that it 
is categorically stated that no such dispute was over raised 
by the workman side with the Management and as such 
this issue is decided in favour of the Management.

CHARGE SHEET 


Please show cause why disciplinary action should not be 

taken against you for the following charges : 


( 1 ) That on the 8th September, 1976 , you came into 

my chamber at 10 . 45 A . M . and on being asked 
by me to mark your attendance in the late atten 
dunce register , you shouted at me and attempted to 
forcibly snatch the Daily attendance register from 
my hands. You also assaulted me physically by 
pusbing me towards the wall of my chamber. You , 
thus, displayed disorderly and indecent behaviour 
in the Bank s premises and also willully disobeyed 
my instructions to mark your attendance in the late 
Attendance Register. 


2 . Before I proceed with the discussion of this case I would 
like le trefly place on tho record the facts of the case . Shri M . 
M . Mordal the concerned workman happened to be a clerk in 
the Giridih Branch of the State Bank of India and for some 
time he was Assisant Secretary of the Union . On 8 - 9 - 76 the 
concerned workman camo lato and he leartthat tho attendance 
register had been sent to the chamber of the Branch Manager , 
He then went to the chamber of the Branch Manager and 
wanted to sign the attendanco in the general attendance register . 
The Branch Manager asked hm to sign the attendance in the 
late attendance regiter giving reasons for his late arrival, The 
managenient s case is that in order to ensure punctuality of 
staff. The system was introduced since June, 1976 by a circu 
lar and office order under which late arrivals were to sign late 
attendance register and to explain the reasons for coming late . 
Shri Mondal refused to sign the late attendance register and 
insisted that he should be allowed to sign thc regular attend. 
ance regiter, The Branch Manager refused to do so where upon 
Shri Mondal began shouting, hurling abuses in filthy language 
and attempted to snatch the attendanco register from the hands 
of the manager. In that process the manager was violently 
pushed and he staggered to the wall and got some injury . The 
late attendance register was ovenu ly snatched from the hands 
of the manager and the workman thereafter signed the attend 
ance register . On 10 - 9 - 76 heing advised by the head office 
the Branch Manager suspended the concerned worknian and 
he futher asked him not to come to the office without ror 
aussion of the catagor, A charge - sheet was issued against him 
210 GI / 81 -- 12

of gross misconduct in terms of paragraph 521( d ) . 
(c ) & (c ) of Sastry Award as retained in tho Desai

branch manager, Shri D . L . Roy , Shn S . Singhal, Shri

within the definition of assault. In the charge - sheet tho

4 . It has been pointed out to me that another clerk Shri 
N . Mukherjee wag similarly charge -sheeted in a separate pro 
ceeding and was also found guilty . Since Shri Mukherjee 
admitted his guilt and begged forglveness of the manage 
mert he was lightly punished . Shri Mondal had however

purpose of digcriminatory attitude on the part of the manage 
ment. But both the cases have to stand on i generate toot 
ing and within the discretion of the management to award 
the quantum of punishment. I therefore do not see that this 
is a case which could be struck down on the ground of 
discrimination ,

fied , for a direction upon the Bank to allot duties of special 
assistant to the concerned workman and for a direction upon 
the Bank to reimburse tho loss sustained by B . K , Nag due to 
malafide transfer . 


4 . According to the Bank s case the reference is not main 
tainable as the Union does not represent the workmen of the 
Bank and that the Union has no authority to aspouse the cause 
of the concerned workman . It is also alleged that according to 
the provisions of the Bipartite Settlement dated 19th October, 
1966 the concerned workman is entitled to get special allow 
ance so long as he discharges the duties of the special assistant, 
The workman was in clerical cadre and he could be withdrawn 
Crom the Clearing House of the Reserve Bank and when he 
ceased to do any additional work or duty of any special assis 
tant as representative of the Clearing House besides his own 
duties as clerk , he could not claim special allowance . The Bank 
is at liberty to transfer the workman to different assignments as 
clerk and he cannot as a matter of right claim to be posted as 
representative of tho Clearing House and to get special allow 
ance. The workman was not a special assistunt and the Bank 
acted legally when he was taken away from his assignment as 
represent tive of the Bank at the Clearing House . There was no 
malice or malafidcs in the conduct of the Bank. According to 
the Bank the warkman concerned can get no relief, 

S . To -day was fixed for hearing of the case . On ihe last 
date the Union did not appear and no step was taken on the 
side of the Union. To -day also the Union has taken no step for 
its absenco. The Bank is present with evidence. Tho Tribunal 
waited for the Union up to 12 Noon . Then the case was taken 
up. The Bank examined one witness J .P . Narayın . He is at pre 
sent Pe: sonnel Officer in the Zonal office of the Bank of 
Madras . At the relevant time he was the Personnel Officer in 
Calcutta Zonal office . From him we get that B . K . Nag iho 
concerned workman was a clerk . That was his substantive 
post, For a period the concerned workman acted as representa 
tive of the Bank at thc Clearing House and at that time besides 
bis own ordinary dutios as clerk ho performod some additional 
duties for which he was given special allowance according to 
tho Bipartite Settlement in view of the Supreme Court deci. 
clon . We also get that the concerned workman was never 
appointed as Special Assistant. He was withdrawn from the 
assignment of the representative at the Clearing House and 
was taken back to his own duties as clerk at the branch of the 
Bank and thereafter he ceased to do any duty of the Special 
Assistant. He had no responsibility of the Special Assistant and 
is such the Bank did not allow the concerned workman spe 
cial allowance. From tho witness we also get that the Bank 
has its own union which is known as Union 
Bank of India Employees Association , West Bengal. 
Almost all the employees of tho Bank in West Bengal ato 
members of the said Union. The witness has further stated 
that the Bengal Bank Workers Organisation is not a union of 
che Bank and the said union has no right to represent the 
workmen of the Bank . 

6 . Tho first question that arises as preliminary point la 
regarding the maintainability of the reforence . We get that the 
Bank has its own union and that almost all the employees of 
the Bank in West Bengal are members of the said union . We 
also get that the Bengal Bank Workers Organisation is not a 
union of the Bank and that it has no right to represent the 
workmen of the Bank , The union does not come to prove that 
it is the representative body and that it has got right to represent 
the workmen of the Bank and to espouse or take up the caso 
of tho workman . The evidence is one sided and in favour of 
tho Bank . It is therefore, clear that the Union at whose in 
stance the reference has been made and which wants to re 
premont the workmen of the Bank has no locus standi or right 
to do the samo, According to the Industrial Disputes Act, tho 
present dispute is not an industrial dispute . The preliminary 
point, therefore , goes in favour of the Bank and in that view of 
the matter tho reference is incompetent. 

7 . In order to make the award complete, I may also deat 
with the merits of the clajn made by the Union . The Union 
relles uinon paragraphs 5 . 2 and 5. 6 of the Ripartite Settlement 
datod 19 - 10 - 1966, When the two paragraphs are read together 
It is quite clear that an employoo in tho clerical cadre as in tho 
present case is entitled to get special allowance for the perfor 
mance or (lischarge of certain additional duties and functions 
over and above the routine duties and functions of the said 
workman . For getting that allowance the workman should con 
tinue to perform the duties whole time and not casually or 
occasionally , Paragraph 5. 9 save that the workman would he 
entitled to special allowance only so tone as he is in charge of 
yuch work or performance of such duties attracting special 


allowance. Now , the duties to be performed for getting special 
allowance have been mentioned in Appendix B to the Bipartita 
Settlement. For a clerk to get special assistant s special allow 
ance he has to do some dutics or any of them as mentioned in 
item ( xix ) of Part I in Appendix B . In the present case B , K . 
Nag the concerned workman was in the clerical cadro and as 
clerk when he was assigned to work as representative of the Bank 
at the Clcaring House he was to do certain duties as mentioned 
in item No. (xix ) in Part I of Appendix B over and above his 
routine and ordinary duties as clerk . This assignment as re 
presentative of the Bank was given to him and ho performed 
the duties as clerk doing some additional duties as mentioned 
in itom No , ( xix ). He was not promoted as Special Assistant, 
Clearly according to the terms of service a cleark can be trert 
ferred from one office to another ind from we assignment to 
another , of course as a clerk . The clerk can not, as a matter 
of right, claim that he should have been allowed to contince 
in thc post where special allowance was given . In the present 
CASC B . K , Nag was taken back to the branch of the Bank as 
clerk and he ccased to be the represenlive of the bank at the 
Clearing House and he did not do any duty as mentioned in 
jtem ( xix ) of Part I of Appendix B of the Bipartite Settlement. 
Therefore, the claim of the Union than the workmanshold he 
reallotted duties as representative of the Bank or that he should 
ho designated as Special Assistant-cum - cleark , which is the cree 
tion of the Union itself because there is no much office in the 
Bank , is unacceptable . There is no evidence of malafides on the 
part of the Bank when thc workman was withdrawn from the 
Clearing House . The action of the Bank in withdrawing 8 . K . 
Nag from the Clcaring House is quite legal and justiflod . 
There is no question of depriving him of the special assirtant s 
allowanco . As. B K . Nag was not required to do any additional 
duties besides his own , he is not entitled to get any srcial 
allowanco. 

8 . I, therefore , find in view of my discussions above that tho 
workman concerned is not entitled to get any relief either on 
preliminary point or on merit . 

This is my award . 
Calcutta , 
Dated , The 11th May , 1981. 

R . BHATTACHARYA , Presiding Officer

AWARD 
The Central Government as appropriate Govcinment vide 
its order No. L - 42012 ( 33 )/ 79 - D . LI( B ) dated the 26th March , 
1980 referred an Indusrtial Dispute u /s 10 of the 1. D , Act, 
1947 to this Tribunal in the following terms ; 
Whether the action of the management of B .S . L . Project 

P . O . Sundernagar Township (HP ) in terminating the 
services of Shri Raj Paul under their letter No. 
53009 / 20 Emergency dated 30 - 11-76 is justified ? 
If not, to what relief the workman is entitled . 


2 . On receipt of the reference ugual notices were sent to 
the parties but none has uppeared for the workman side and 
ay such ex - parte proceddings were ordered against the work 
man . The BSL has put in appearance and filed a statement 
of claim , In view of the statement of claim filed by the BSL 
following issues were framcd vide my order dated 5 - 12 - 80 
and case was adjourned for ex -parle evidence of the BSL . 
Ex- Parte statement of BSL consists of Shri O , P , Yamdagni in 
the manner of an affidavit which is Ex, M / 1 and documents 
Ex. M / 2 to Ex. M / 7 . I have gone through the documents 
produced by the Management and also the evidence and 
after giving my considered thought to the malter before me 
I have come to the following findings ; 


Issues : 

1. As in the order of reference ? 
2 . Whether thc Indusrtial Disputo is not tenablo ? 


Issue No. 1 : 

According to the statement of claim filed by the Manage 
ment Raj Paul was employed as a Timo Keeper with the res 
pondent and his services were terminated in 45 much as he 
was irregular in attendance and his CRs also did not warrant his 
continuance in the service. It is further stated in the state 
ment of claim by the BSL that Raj Paul was a Timo Keeper 
and was not a workman in as much as he was govorned by 
Civil service rules and as such this reference was bad . 


18 . In view of my findings upon issue no, 1 I hold that the 
Management of Union Bank of India is justified in dismis 
sing from service w .e .f . 18th June, 1974 Shri B . R . Trivedi, 
Head Cashier , Aligarh Branch and that ihę woshman was 
not entitled to any relief whatsoever . In this context I 
would like to refer to the charges levelled against the work 
man , Charge 110 . 1 was that on 16th June , 1972 at about 
11.00 AM Mr. Tiivedi (workman ) entered the cabin of Mr. 
N , L. Shurma, Bianch Manager and behaved with him in a 
rude and arroyant manner . In charge no . 2 workman was 
charged with having abused and threatened the Branch 
Manager and attempted to assault the Branch Manager with 
4 wooden tray . Under charge no , 3 Mr. Trivedi had been 
charged with asking on 12 - 6 -72 Mr. P . K . Sharma, another 
employee in the bank to handover a lctler to Miss Pushpi 
Kaul in which puper contained indiscieet writing addressed to 
the lady staff member . Charge no, 4 and 6 were dropped . In 
charge no . 5 Mr, Trivedi has been charged with approaching 
on 23 - 6 -72 at 3 .30 PM the accountant with a complaint re 
garding the iron gate and behaved in an indecent and im 
moral manner in the presence of other staff member and on 
being advised by the accountant to behave in a decent and 
proper manner, Mr. Trivedi started abusing the accountant . 
Kecping in view the nature of the charges it cannot be said 
that the punishment of dismissal from service was unwarrant 
ed and is not justified . Even a reference to Ex . W 53 which 
rc -produced certain language used by the workman also do 
not go to show that the punishment of dismissal in not war 
ranted in thc instant case . Even a persal of Ex. M / 1 the 
enclosure of Ex. 15 would show that the workman was haidly 
a person deserving a retention in the service in an establish 
ment where women employees were working Thọ couplet 
written on Ex. Mi tends to violate the modesty of any wo 
man more 80 of a respectablo employce in a banking estab 
lishment and keeping in view all these facts I do not think 
that the order of termination of services of the workman is 
unjustified and in view of my discussions and findings above , 
it is awarded that the Management of Union Bank of India 
in terminating the services of Shri B , R Trivedl was justified 
w . e.f, 18 -6 -74 from the post of Head Cashier, Aligarh Branch 
and the workman is not entitled to any relief. However, in 
the circumstances of the case I leave the particg to bear their 
own costs. 
Further ordered : 

That requisite number of copies of this award inay be 
sent to the appropriato Govt. for necessary action at their 
end . 
Dated : the 13th April, 1981.

3 . In order to support its contention the Management hos 
filed an affiadavit of Superintending Engineer which is Ex . 
M / 1 and from the perusal thereof I find that by and large 
the contention of the Management raised in the statement of 
claim has been repealed in the said affidavit Ex . M / 1 . Ex, 
M / 2 is the notice served upon Shri Raj Pau by the Executive 
Engineer on 21 - 5 -70 warning him to be careful in future , Ex . 
M / 3 is the copy of report for 1975 - 76 of this workman , Ex , 
M / 4 is the notice on the basis of the report Ex. M / 3 issued to 
the workman on 4 - 11- 76 . Ex. M / S is the receipt thereof. Ex. 
M /6 is another report for the period Juno, 1976 to December , 
1976 immediately before his services were terminat 
ed and Ex. M / 7 is copy of d .o . letter dated 25 - 8 - 73 . 
From the perusa ) of the affidavit of BSL it cannot be said 
that Shri Raj Paul was not a workman as alleged by BSL . 
Admittedly Shri Raj Paul was working as a Time Keeper . It 
is also admitted that the respondent is an Industry . That being 
tho position mere fact that Ral Paul was governed by Civil 
Service Rules would not exclude him from the category of a 
workman . Keeping in view the designation of Raj Paul who 
was & Timo Keeper it would essentially follow that he was 
concerned with the charge staff and was part of work chilgo 
staff . As long as he was a part of work chargo staff he would 
be a workman . There is nothing to suggest that he was 
working in a supervisory capacity or that his wages were more 
than Rs. 500 P . M . in that supervisory capacity . Keeping 
in view All these facts it would follow that tho conclusion 
would be irresistable that Raj Paul was a workman . A cyclo 
styled Judgment of Hon ble Supreme Court of India in Writ 
Petition No . 3598 , 4360 , 4429 , 4536 , 4391 . 4505 , 4376 , 
4658 of 1978 and 565 of 1979 passed on 29 - 8 -79 entitled as 
Taswant Singh and others Vs. Union of India and others has 
been filed by the Management. From the poruşal of this copy 
itselt it would be clear that the cntire strength of labour emp 
loyed for the Project was work charge and as such they were 
governed by the piovisions of Industrial Dispute Act, 1947 . 
Onco it is held that the workmun was governed by the provin 
sions of Industrial Dispuite Act it would follow further that the 
provisions of Section 25- F would be equally applicable to the 
vions of Industrial Dispute Act it would follow further that the 
in service of the respondent - BST for a period of more than

1. विनिविष्ट विवावग्रस्त विषय --- - 


one year and there is nothing to suggest that this workman was 
paid any retrenchment compensation within the meaning of 
Section 25- F of I. D . Act. This iman was in service with the 
respondent w . c. f . 2 - 11 - 67 and remained in service till 1976 
and his services were terminated w . e.f . 30 - 11 - 76 . He was not 
paid any retrenchment compensation and hence in view of the 
principles of law laid down in N . Sundermany s case by the 
Hon ble Supreme Court of India termination of his services 
would be illegal. Mere fact that the service rules applicable 
to this workman provided that his services could be terminated 
without notice at any time docs not in any manner do away 
with the requirement of payment of retrenchment compenya 
tion and hence termination was in violation of law and there 
fore illegal and this issue is decided accordingly .

was a workman and provisions of I. D . Act were applicable to 
him it cannot be said that the reference is not maintainable 
and accordingly this issuc is also decided against the Manage 
ment.

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 28th May , 1981 
$ . 0 1693 — Whereas an industrial dispute cxists between 
the employers in relation to the management of Bombay Port 
Trust, Bombay and their workmen represented by BPT Genorul 
Workors Union , 

And whercas the said employers and their workmen have 
by a written agreenient under sub section (1) cf section 10A of 
the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 (14 of 1947), agreed to refer 
the said dispute to arbitration and have forwarded 10 thc Cçn
